# SP38_CW_75 — changes and decisions

Counts are distinct adjudicated loci, not changed tokens. A locus may differ from both witnesses. C changes include added, resolved or reordered doubt marks; these editorial updates are not all transcription errors. Unchanged retained C doubts and witness-S checks are listed but not counted as changes. H macro-only differences, confidence-only changes with the same reading, editorial page markers and the untranscribed cover are excluded. Confidence totals count each changed locus once.

## Consequential corrections

- Scan 3, Box: subscript of second u: H regularizes the boxed subscript to sx; the ink has gx, retaining the mismatch with the later s-based calculation.
- Scan 3, Box: composition and question mark before int: The boxed composition carries a live authorial question mark omitted by H; it is not an editorial uncertainty mark.
- Scan 3, Cancelled long equation: int(s) exponent: The canceled trial formula has int(s), with no inverse exponent; H introduces an inverse that is not on the page.
- Scan 4, Sign after universal-quantifier clause: The extra existential sign is syntactically unexpected before a cardinality equality. It remains because the ink is present; mathematics is not used to delete it.
- Scan 4, Small diagram to right of bracketed paragraph: The cap can express containment vertically, but that meaning does not license replacing the drawn sign by an arrow.
- Scan 5, Existence line, relation before E: The weak written relation is preserved although the surrounding statement concerns an embedding.
- Scan 5, Existence line, character after qui: The resulting wording qui f prolonge is retained as written.
- Scan 5, Connectivity definition after second equals: qui n'est pas
- Scan 6, De plus: raised sign on Aut: The superscript identifying the purported cyclic subgroup is letterlike and unresolved; C/H’s unmarked + is not forced into the ink.
- Scan 7, Second letter in the pair with exponent minus one: The second base in the pair is retained as s|g, with s preferred from its short shape. The inverse-pair cardinality check applies only to the g alternative.
- Scan 9, Arrow after ordre circulaire; sweep SA01-K75-01: Actually checked the asserted sufficiency with u=(12)(34): n_2=2 and all other n_i=0, yet u has two orbits. The manuscript equivalence is false; the written criterion is only necessary for nonempty transitivity.
- Scan 9, First chain, mark after terminal 1: 1q
- Scan 9, Identity annotation, left flanking mark: Keep the extra first character and the clear qp order. A usual-composition type check identifies the mismatch but cannot repair the flanking overwritings.
- Scan 9, Right note, canceled object after de: The crossed base favors R, with Gamma retained. H supplies a separate live Gamma that is not securely present; the expected quotient is not supplied.
- Scan 9, Rectification parenthesis: The written reason is car on ne veut pas de polygone à 1 coté. H substitutes different wording; the crop supports the fixed-point exclusion as written.
- Scan 9, Second chain target in right annotation: The live target Gamma over a canceled iso/is is retained; H lacks that target and its overwrite state.
- Scan 9, Left note, affine-group formula and intervening prose: The note has Aff(Z) and a semidirect-product-like expression, not H squared of Gamma,Z. The low point is retained; the preceding compressed words remain separately qualified.
- Scan 10, i(g), raised glyph above tau: The translation superscript and its deletion state remain unresolved. A usual inverse right-translation convention checks the sequence, but is not supplied to the text.
- Scan 10, Live NB, torseur hypothesis: Both primes are in the ink: the target E′ is a G′-torsor. H loses the primes and thereby changes the uniqueness hypothesis; M010-UNIQ verifies the written statement and M010-SOURCE refutes H’s altered version.
- Scan 11, Second q subscript in the first live proof row: The ink favors b|t at the second q-subscript. A general q_b need not fix t; the conditional counterexample and the q_t refinement are recorded separately in M011-INDEX and M011-CHANGE.
- Scan 12, NB fibre sizes: The ink says vides ou de cardinal 2, not H’s toutes de cardinal 2. The n=4 certificate refutes the latter statement for fibres over the full reflection coset; see M012-H-EVEN and M012-N4-CERT.
- Scan 13, Top diagram, label right of Z-to-Z upward arrow: H supplies id although the ink remains 2|wr. Splitting follows for the pullback/kernel-isomorphism interpretation; M013-ARBITRARY gives a counterexample for an unrestricted extension morphism. The mathematics does not select the label.
- Scan 14, affine structures, target of Isom: SA01-K75-02 confirmed from ink: prose E versus Isom target V. Retain V. M014-AFF-MIXED refutes the unqualified mixed-base classification; refined M014-AFF-STRUCT uses E on both sides.

### Unresolved

- Opening contracted determiner: tte; alternatives: toute (medium).
- Overwritten origin sign: \omega; alternatives: o (low).
- Canceled existential in margin: \exists1; alternatives: \exists (medium).

### Unresolved

- Heading sign: :; alternatives: à; two detached points, no secure a body (low).
- Heading contraction: grp; alternatives: grpe (medium).
- Recurring upper group alphabet: \mathbb Z; alternatives: Z; later local repetitions use the primary form (medium).
- Canceled opening expression: Aut with two lower G labels and one D/T argument, mapping to Aut(G); alternatives: overall structure partly covered by crossing (low).
- Canceled second lower label: var; alternatives: op (low).
- Canceled argument: D; alternatives: T (low).
- Detached trace above un: short horizontal dark trace; alternatives: intentional writing versus incidental ink/surface trace (low).
- Verb before t-prime: avait; alternatives: aurait (medium).
- Verification joining mark: \circ; alternatives: curled return of the opening parenthesis, not a separate sign (low).
- Second q-subscript in live proof: b; alternatives: t (medium).
- NB short word: si; alternatives: compressed undivided strokes; no competing word secured (medium).
